BEFORE the sweep'. Every one of these is a symbol. A threshold that is named but not valued is not pre-registered -- it can be set after the layer-by-position maps are seen, which is exactly the freedom the rest of the document works hard to remove. The same applies to the two quantitative gates that DO have numbers (the distinct-3-gram ratio of 0.6 and the similarity gap of 0.15 in the template-collapse diagnostic, and the +/-25% TOST margin for the placebo): those are the model for what the rest should look like, and they show the author knows how to do it.\",", "+      \"suggested_action\": \"Add a criteria table to success_criteria with one row per pass/fail test and a NUMBER in the threshold column, expressed in the standardized null-SD units of the previous critique so the values are interpretable before any data exist: e.g. safety-specificity margin = 1.0 null-SD; delta_1 = delta_2 = 1.0 null-SD; delta_3 = 0.75 null-SD; TOST equivalence margin for post-edit A = +/-0.5 null-SD; split-half cosine pass threshold = 0.7. Meanwhile the whole of Stage 1 is stated as 256 forward passes with zero generated tokens on a 20 GB card -- seconds of GPU time. There is no resource argument for the number that determines every interval, every margin and every MDE in the design. The document's own escape hatch ('if 24 scenarios do not reach it, the scenario count is raised before Stage 1 runs') is conditioned only on the split-half direction-stability check, not on power for A.\",", "+      \"suggested_action\": \"Set the item count from a power calculation for the COHERENCE-NET ARMING TERM at the smallest effect you would be willing to call real (state it in null-SD units), not from what the twin sets happen to yield, and report the resulting n and MDE in the hypothesis. If the matched-twin supply is the limiting factor rather than compute, say so explicitly and state the largest attainable n and the MDE it delivers -- an honest 'this design can detect A >= X and no smaller' is worth more than an unqualified n=24. Subtracting the coherence interaction from the safety interaction is valid only if (a) the generic instruction-mismatch signal enters the readout additively and (b) the two crossings produce mismatches of comparable MAGNITUDE. Mechanical generation is the right call for auditability and it answers the previous round's probe-set critique, but it buys reproducibility at the cost of generality: the standing rule in this field is that a result reported from one input distribution is a single draw from an equivalence class rather than a property, and the stability criterion as written (folds, bootstrap resamples, probe-set subsamples) re-samples the SAME template.         c['description'] += " And the item supply is not the limit either: XSTest, the resource the design anchors on, ships 250 safe prompts with 200 unsafe CONTRAST prompts, so on the order of 200 twin pairs are available before OR-Bench is touched. Twenty-four is therefore a choice, not a constraint, and it is the choice that sets every interval in the study."
        c['suggested_action'] = c['suggested_action'].replace(
            "If the matched-twin supply is the limiting factor rather than compute, say so explicitly and state the largest attainable n and the MDE it delivers -- an honest 'this design can detect A >= X and no smaller' is worth more than an unqualified n=24.",
            "The matched-twin supply is not the limiting factor (XSTest alone offers ~200 contrast pairs), so if you keep a small n, state the reason explicitly and report the MDE it delivers -- an honest 'this design can detect A >= X and no smaller' is worth more than an unqualified n=24.")
json.dump(d,open(p,'w'),indent=2)
print('patched')
EOF
```

Reviewed iter-2 hypothesis "Safety only checks output when warned" (O / CB / A = arming interaction) for run_YqmEFECOIR3D on 2026-09-20. Verdict **5 / borderline**, confidence 4; fidelity 4, soundness 3, presentation 3, contribution 3; H↔H relation = **embedding** (old (O,C) is the special case CB+A at one request level).

**All 21 iter-1 critiques were addressed** — SRS deleted, subspace patching rung, crossed coherence control, cross-fitting, 5-of-6 family rule, co-primary harmful-compliance, XSTest-anchored probe set, parent-fixed basis. Do not re-raise them.

**7 new majors, in impact order:**
1. **ANNIHILATION RISK (highest).** The primary harm-axis readout never states its fitting corpus or POSITIONS. If it is diff-of-means over harmful/harmless *requests at prompt positions*, it IS the abliteration direction, so weight orthogonalisation zeroes that residual component everywhere and the parent-fixed post-edit O/CB/A are an algebraic residue. Fix: fit at RESPONSE positions; pre-register cos(r_readout, r_ablit) gate; add a variance-along-r-vs-random-directions diagnostic.
2. Headline part (iii) `CB_post(SafeRL) > CB_post(Qwen3-4B)` is **entailed by P2 + part (ii)** — it tests level, not survival. Fix: difference-in-differences `[CB_post−CB_pre](SafeRL) − [CB_post−CB_pre](Qwen3)`, no denominator.
3. **"Absolute internal units" are NOT cross-checkpoint commensurable** (residual/LayerNorm scales differ and move under the edit). This is an over-correction from iter-1's anti-ratio push. Fix: standardise by each checkpoint's OWN measured null-band SD (already computed from the ≥20 random-direction draws).
4. Pre-registration has **zero numbers** — every delta/TOST margin is a symbol.
5. **n=24** for an interaction (4× variance), net of a second interaction, under 6-fold cross-fitting — while Stage 1 is only 256 forward passes. XSTest ships 250 safe / **200 unsafe contrast** prompts, so ~200 twin pairs exist; 24 is a choice, not a constraint. Ask ≥96.
6. Coherence subtraction assumes additivity + matched mismatch magnitude. Fix: calibrate both 2×2s on the model's own **prefix NLL given request** (free from the same passes); regress rather than subtract if unmatched.
7. "Internal refusal drive" (outcome of the causal arm AND half the safety-specificity gate) is **never defined**.

**Verified facts (useful downstream):**
- arXiv:2607.14147 exists (arxiv.org/html/2607.14147v1). Its pre-registered 2×2 is harm × **SURFACE trigger-word density** (AdvBench-scary/clean × XSTest-safe/Alpaca) — NOT harm × prefill. It never puts hazardous-CONTENT continuation under a benign request ⇒ **the arming interaction is genuinely OPEN**.
- Its exact quotes: "+16.8pp" specificity DiD is behavioural, attached to an early-attention knockout (benign 0%→4%); base-model control = "generic autoregressive conditioning"; benign-residual patch also restores refusal to 100% at every layer; "any single direction reads ≤0.73 and none steers" vs full probe "∼0.85"; harm-probe reads refuse-vs-comply only **AUC 0.63–0.72**; concentration 0.24 (instruct) vs 0.03 (base). All quoted accurately by the hypothesis.
- arXiv:2505.19056 confirmed (KAUST; Llama-2-7B-Chat + Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B): ≤10% vs 70–80% refusal drops, plus prompt-centroid PCA section.
- Knowledge-action-gap source the hypothesis cites only by description = **arXiv:2603.18353** (98.2% AUROC probes vs 45.1% output sensitivity; Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, clinical triage).
- **Ungated non-safety fine-tunes of Qwen3-4B-Base DO exist** (5th-arm control is available, cross-family fallback unnecessary): `HikariLight/Qwen3_4B_Base__COMP_ACI_DAMT_SFT_Merged`, `shjondhale/AzureML-Qwen3-4B-Base-GRPO`, `CohenQu/Qwen3-4B-Base_HintGen-STaR.03.01_1e-6`, several `SVRL/...webinstruct-verified_Qwen3-4B-Base`.
- Mech-interp handbook rules the hypothesis already satisfies (don't re-raise): decodability≠actionability gate, collateral disruption, per-item distributions, diff-in-means + raw hidden vectors as baselines, measured nulls, stability across draws.

See [[project-qwen3-safety-genhypo-iter2-run-yqmefecoir3d]] and [[project-qwen3-safety-reviewhypo-run-yqmefecoir3d]].
```

Then: no numeric thresholds anywhere in the pre-registration; n=24 for an interaction in a design whose Stage 1 is 256 forward passes; the coherence subtraction's unchecked additivity; and "internal refusal drive" left undefined despite carrying half the safety-specificity gate.
